<!DOCTYPE html>  
<html lang="en">  
<head>  
<meta charset="UTF-8">  
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">  
<title>HASH72 ⊗ Lo Shu Harmonic Explorer</title>  
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:ital,wght@0,400;0,600;0,700;1,400&family=Fira+Code:wght@300;400;500&family=Spectral:wght@300;400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">  
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css">  
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>  
<style>  
*,*::before,*::after{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}  
:root{  
  --bg-deep:#06080F;--bg-primary:#0C1017;--bg-card:#151A24;--bg-hover:#1C2230;  
  --accent-gold:#C9A84C;--accent-teal:#3ABFB0;--accent-rose:#C45B5B;  
  --text-primary:#E8DFD0;--text-secondary:#8B8078;--text-dim:#555049;  
  --border:#2A2520;--font-display:'Cormorant Garamond',serif;  
  --font-mono:'Fira Code',monospace;--font-body:'Spectral',serif;  
  --s9-1:#D4A574;--s9-2:#6BA3C7;--s9-3:#5AAF6E;--s9-4:#4ECDC4;  
  --s9-5:#C45B5B;--s9-6:#9B7DCF;--s9-7:#F0E6D3;--s9-8:#E08A4E;--s9-9:#C27BA0;  
}  
html.light{  
  --bg-deep:#F0EBE0;--bg-primary:#FAF6EE;--bg-card:#FFFFFF;--bg-hover:#EDE7DA;  
  --accent-gold:#A07820;--accent-teal:#1A8F82;--accent-rose:#B03535;  
  --text-primary:#1A140A;--text-secondary:#6B5F50;--text-dim:#A09585;  
  --border:#D0C8B8;  
  --s9-1:#A07820;--s9-2:#4A7FA0;--s9-3:#3A8A4E;--s9-4:#1A8F82;  
  --s9-5:#B03535;--s9-6:#7A5FAF;--s9-7:#5A4A30;--s9-8:#C06A2E;--s9-9:#A05B80;  
}  
html,body{height:100%;overflow:hidden;background:var(--bg-deep);color:var(--text-primary);font-family:var(--font-body)}  
#app{display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:100%;overflow:hidden}  
header{padding:10px 16px;background:var(--bg-primary);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);flex-shrink:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}  
.logo{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold);white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:1px}  
.logo span{color:var(--accent-teal);font-weight:400}  
nav{display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-left:auto}  
nav button{background:none;border:1px solid transparent;color:var(--text-secondary);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s;white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:0.5px}  
nav button:hover{color:var(--text-primary);background:var(--bg-hover)}  
nav button.active{color:var(--accent-gold);border-color:var(--accent-gold);background:rgba(201,168,76,0.08)}  
.tab-content{flex:1;overflow:hidden;position:relative}  
.tab-panel{display:none;width:100%;height:100%;overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden}  
.tab-panel.active{display:block}  
/* TORUS TAB */  
#torus-panel{overflow:hidden;position:relative}  
#torus-canvas{width:100%;height:100%;display:block}  
#torus-info{position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;background:rgba(12,16,23,0.92);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:14px;max-width:280px;font-size:0.82rem;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;transition:opacity .3s;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}  
html.light #torus-info{background:rgba(250,246,238,0.92)}  
#torus-info.visible{opacity:1}  
#torus-info .ti-hex{font-size:2.4rem;text-align:center;line-height:1}  
#torus-info .ti-name{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.2rem;color:var(--accent-gold);text-align:center;margin-top:4px}  
#torus-info .ti-idx{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center}  
#torus-info .ti-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--accent-teal);text-align:center;margin-top:6px}  
#torus-info .ti-phase{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--accent-teal);text-align:center;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.35;background:var(--bg-hover);border-radius:4px;padding:4px 6px}  
#torus-info .ti-gate{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.64rem;color:var(--text-dim);text-align:center;margin-top:4px;line-height:1.3}  
#torus-info .ti-div{font-family:var(--font-body);font-style:italic;font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.3}  
#torus-legend{position:absolute;bottom:12px;left:12px;background:rgba(12,16,23,0.85);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 14px;font-size:0.68rem;backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}  
html.light #torus-legend{background:rgba(250,246,238,0.85)}  
#torus-legend .leg-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin:3px 0}  
#torus-legend .leg-dot{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0}  
#torus-hint{position:absolute;bottom:12px;right:12px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-dim);background:rgba(12,16,23,0.7);padding:6px 10px;border-radius:4px}  
html.light #torus-hint{background:rgba(250,246,238,0.7)}  
/* CODEX TAB */  
.codex-header{padding:12px 16px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--bg-primary);position:sticky;top:0;z-index:10}  
.codex-search{flex:1;min-width:180px;padding:8px 12px;background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;color:var(--text-primary);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:16px;outline:none;transition:border-color .2s}  
.codex-search:focus{border-color:var(--accent-gold)}  
.codex-filters{display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap}  
.s9-btn{width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid transparent;cursor:pointer;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.65rem;font-weight:600;color:#000;transition:all .2s;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center}  
.s9-btn.active{border-color:#fff;transform:scale(1.15);box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(201,168,76,0.5)}  
.codex-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:12px;padding:16px}  
.codex-card{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:14px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .25s;position:relative;overflow:hidden}  
.codex-card:hover{border-color:var(--accent-gold);transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)}  
.codex-card::before{content:'';position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:4px;height:100%;border-radius:4px 0 0 4px}  
.cc-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:8px}  
.cc-hex{font-size:1.8rem;line-height:1}  
.cc-idx{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-dim);background:var(--bg-hover);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px}  
.cc-name{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:4px}  
.cc-tri{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.85rem;letter-spacing:2px}  
.cc-wave{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-secondary);letter-spacing:1px;margin:6px 0}  
.cc-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.cc-eli5{font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-secondary);font-style:italic;margin-top:6px}  
/* DETAIL MODAL */  
.modal-overlay{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(6,8,15,0.85);z-index:100;justify-content:center;align-items:center;padding:16px}  
.modal-overlay.open{display:flex}  
.modal{background:var(--bg-primary);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;max-width:540px;width:100%;max-height:85vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:24px;position:relative;animation:modalIn .3s ease}  
@keyframes modalIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(20px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}  
.modal-close{position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;background:none;border:none;color:var(--text-secondary);font-size:1.2rem;cursor:pointer;padding:4px 8px}  
.modal-close:hover{color:var(--text-primary)}  
.md-hex{font-size:3.5rem;text-align:center;line-height:1;margin-bottom:4px}  
.md-name{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold);text-align:center}  
.md-sub{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;margin-bottom:16px}  
.md-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-bottom:16px}  
.md-field{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:10px}  
.md-field-label{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text-dim);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;margin-bottom:4px}  
.md-field-val{font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-primary);word-break:break-word}  
.md-field.full{grid-column:1/-1}  
.md-div{font-family:var(--font-body);font-style:italic;font-size:0.9rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;padding:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);line-height:1.5}  
/* LO SHU TAB */  
.loshu-wrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:24px 16px;gap:24px}  
.loshu-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold)}  
.loshu-subtitle{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-secondary)}  
.loshu-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:6px;width:min(340px,90vw);aspect-ratio:1}  
.ls-cell{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .3s;position:relative}  
.ls-cell:hover{border-color:var(--accent-gold);transform:scale(1.04)}  
.ls-cell.active{border-color:var(--accent-gold);box-shadow:0 0 20px rgba(201,168,76,0.25)}  
.ls-num{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:2.4rem;font-weight:700;line-height:1}  
.ls-label{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:4px}  
.loshu-sums{display:flex;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center}  
.loshu-sum{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 10px;background:var(--bg-card);border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--border)}  
.loshu-sum span{color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.loshu-detail{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;max-width:500px;width:100%;min-height:120px}  
.loshu-detail h3{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.2rem;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:8px}  
.loshu-states{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}  
.ls-state-row{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font-size:0.78rem;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--bg-hover)}  
.ls-state-row .ls-sr-idx{font-family:var(--font-mono);color:var(--text-dim);width:24px;flex-shrink:0}  
.ls-state-row .ls-sr-hex{font-size:1.1rem;flex-shrink:0}  
.ls-state-row .ls-sr-name{color:var(--accent-gold);font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:600}  
.ls-state-row .ls-sr-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--accent-teal);margin-left:auto}  
/* CIRCLE OF FIFTHS TAB */  
.fifths-wrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:24px 16px;gap:16px}  
.fifths-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold)}  
.fifths-sub{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center}  
.fifths-svg-wrap{width:min(420px,90vw);aspect-ratio:1;position:relative}  
.fifths-info{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;max-width:500px;width:100%;font-size:0.82rem}  
.fifths-info h4{font-family:var(--font-display);color:var(--accent-gold);font-size:1.1rem;margin-bottom:8px}  
.fifths-info .fi-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}  
.fifths-info .fi-row:last-child{border:none}  
.fifths-info .fi-label{font-family:var(--font-mono);color:var(--text-secondary);font-size:0.72rem}  
.fifths-info .fi-val{font-family:var(--font-mono);color:var(--accent-teal);font-size:0.78rem}  
/* GATE TAB */  
.gate-wrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:24px 16px;gap:20px;max-width:700px;margin:0 auto}  
.gate-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold)}  
.gate-section{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;width:100%}  
.gate-section h3{font-family:var(--font-display);color:var(--accent-gold);font-size:1.1rem;margin-bottom:10px}  
.gate-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--accent-teal);padding:8px;background:var(--bg-hover);border-radius:4px;margin:6px 0;overflow-x:auto;white-space:nowrap}  
.gate-status{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px}  
.gate-chip{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:20px;border:1px solid var(--border)}  
.gate-chip.pass{border-color:var(--s9-3);color:var(--s9-3)}  
.gate-chip.fail{border-color:var(--accent-rose);color:var(--accent-rose)}  
.gate-chip.witness{border-color:#E08A4E;color:#E08A4E;background:rgba(224,138,78,0.08)}  
.gate-chip.axiom{border-color:var(--accent-teal);color:var(--accent-teal);font-style:italic}  
.gate-chip .gc-mode{font-size:0.58rem;opacity:0.7;margin-right:2px}  
.gate-matrix{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:4px;margin:10px 0}  
.gate-matrix-cell{background:var(--bg-hover);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;padding:8px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-primary)}  
.gate-inputs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:8px;margin:10px 0}  
.gate-input-group{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px}  
.gate-input-group label{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px}  
.gate-input-group input{padding:8px;background:var(--bg-hover);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;color:var(--text-primary);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:16px;outline:none}  
.gate-input-group input:focus{border-color:var(--accent-gold)}  
.gate-btn{padding:8px 20px;background:var(--accent-gold);color:#000;border:none;border-radius:6px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;transition:all .2s;margin-top:8px}  
.gate-btn:hover{filter:brightness(1.15)}  
/* SCROLLBAR */  
::-webkit-scrollbar{width:6px}  
::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:var(--bg-deep)}  
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:var(--border);border-radius:3px}  
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover{background:var(--text-dim)}  
/* ANIMATIONS */  
@keyframes fadeUp{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(12px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}  
.codex-card{animation:fadeUp .4s ease backwards}  
/* SCHEMA TAB */  
.schema-wrap{padding:24px 16px;max-width:760px;margin:0 auto}  
.schema-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold);text-align:center;margin-bottom:4px}  
.schema-sub{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px}  
.axiom-card{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:12px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;border-left:3px solid var(--accent-gold)}  
.axiom-card.teal{border-left-color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.axiom-card.rose{border-left-color:var(--accent-rose)}  
.axiom-tag{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--accent-gold);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1.5px;margin-bottom:6px}  
.axiom-card.teal .axiom-tag{color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.axiom-card.rose .axiom-tag{color:var(--accent-rose)}  
.axiom-name{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.15rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:6px}  
.axiom-body{font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-secondary);line-height:1.55}  
.axiom-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--accent-teal);background:var(--bg-hover);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:4px;margin:8px 0;display:inline-block;max-width:100%;overflow-x:auto}  
.axiom-note{font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-style:italic;margin-top:6px}  
.schema-divider{border:none;border-top:1px dashed var(--border);margin:20px 0}  
.schema-section-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-teal);margin-bottom:12px}  
/* CUBIC TAB */  
.cubic-wrap{padding:24px 16px;max-width:760px;margin:0 auto}  
.cubic-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold);text-align:center;margin-bottom:4px}  
.cubic-sub{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px}  
#cubic-canvas{width:100%;max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;display:block;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;background:var(--bg-card);cursor:crosshair}  
.cubic-info{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;margin-top:16px}  
.cubic-info-card{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:14px}  
.cubic-info-card h4{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1rem;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:8px}  
.cubic-info-card .ci-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--accent-teal);margin:4px 0}  
.cubic-info-card .ci-val{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-primary);margin:3px 0}  
.cubic-info-card .ci-note{font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);font-style:italic;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.4}  
.cubic-roots{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;margin-top:12px}  
.cubic-roots h4{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1rem;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:10px}  
.root-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:8px;border-radius:4px;margin-bottom:6px;background:var(--bg-hover)}  
.root-dot{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0}  
.root-label{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);width:28px;flex-shrink:0}  
.root-value{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-primary);flex:1}  
.root-desc{font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-dim)}  
.cubic-mouse-info{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text-dim);text-align:center;margin-top:8px}  
/* PHYSICS TAB */  
.physics-wrap{padding:24px 16px;max-width:760px;margin:0 auto}  
.physics-title{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-gold);text-align:center;margin-bottom:4px}  
.physics-sub{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px}  
.phys-table{width:100%;border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:16px}  
.phys-table th{background:var(--bg-card);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--accent-gold);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;padding:10px 12px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}  
.phys-table td{padding:10px 12px;font-size:0.8rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);vertical-align:top}  
.phys-table tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}  
.phys-table tr:hover td{background:var(--bg-hover)}  
.phys-table .phys-hhs{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.phys-table .phys-std{color:var(--text-primary)}  
.phys-table .phys-reason{font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);font-style:italic}  
.phys-section{background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:16px}  
.phys-section h3{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:1.15rem;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:10px}  
.phys-compare{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:16px}  
.phys-col{padding:14px}  
.phys-col:first-child{border-right:1px solid var(--border)}  
.phys-col h4{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;margin-bottom:10px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}  
.phys-col.classical h4{color:var(--accent-rose)}  
.phys-col.hhs h4{color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.phys-row{font-size:0.78rem;padding:4px 0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between}  
.phys-row .pr-label{color:var(--text-secondary)}  
.phys-row .pr-val{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem}  
.phys-col.classical .pr-val{color:var(--accent-rose)}  
.phys-col.hhs .pr-val{color:var(--accent-teal)}  
.phys-seal{text-align:center;padding:16px;background:var(--bg-card);border:1px solid var(--accent-gold);border-radius:8px;margin-top:16px}  
.phys-seal-eq{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.85rem;color:var(--accent-gold);margin-bottom:6px}  
.phys-seal-note{font-family:var(--font-body);font-style:italic;font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-secondary)}  
/* RESPONSIVE */  
@media(max-width:600px){  
  header{padding:8px 10px}  
  .logo{font-size:1rem}  
  nav button{font-size:0.65rem;padding:5px 8px}  
  .codex-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;padding:10px}  
  .loshu-grid{width:min(280px,90vw)}  
  .md-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}  
  .gate-inputs{grid-template-columns:1fr}  
  .cubic-info{grid-template-columns:1fr}  
  .phys-compare{grid-template-columns:1fr}  
  .phys-col:first-child{border-right:none;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}  
}  
</style>  
</head>  
<body>  
<div id="app">  
<header>  
  <div class="logo">HASH72 <span>⊗ Lo Shu</span></div>  
  <nav id="main-nav">  
    <button class="active" data-tab="torus"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch"></i> Torus</button>  
    <button data-tab="codex"><i class="fas fa-scroll"></i> Codex</button>  
    <button data-tab="loshu"><i class="fas fa-th"></i> Lo Shu</button>  
    <button data-tab="fifths"><i class="fas fa-sync-alt"></i> Fifths</button>  
    <button data-tab="gate"><i class="fas fa-lock"></i> Gate</button>  
    <button data-tab="cubic"><i class="fas fa-chart-line"></i> Cubic</button>  
    <button data-tab="schema"><i class="fas fa-book"></i> Schema</button>  
    <button data-tab="physics"><i class="fas fa-atom"></i> Physics</button>  
  </nav>  
</header>  
<div class="tab-content">  
  <!-- TORUS TAB -->  
  <div id="torus-panel" class="tab-panel active">  
    <canvas id="torus-canvas"></canvas>  
    <div id="torus-info">  
      <div class="ti-hex" id="ti-hex"></div>  
      <div class="ti-name" id="ti-name"></div>  
      <div class="ti-idx" id="ti-idx"></div>  
      <div class="ti-phase" id="ti-phase"></div>  
      <div class="ti-eq" id="ti-eq"></div>  
      <div class="ti-gate" id="ti-gate"></div>  
      <div class="ti-div" id="ti-div"></div>  
    </div>  
    <div id="torus-legend"></div>  
    <div id="torus-hint">drag to rotate · scroll to zoom · click node for info</div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- CODEX TAB -->  
  <div id="codex-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="codex-header">  
      <input type="text" class="codex-search" id="codex-search" placeholder="Search states...">  
      <div class="codex-filters" id="codex-filters"></div>  
    </div>  
    <div class="codex-grid" id="codex-grid"></div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- LO SHU TAB -->  
  <div id="loshu-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="loshu-wrap">  
      <div class="loshu-title">Lo Shu Magic Square</div>  
      <div class="loshu-subtitle">Σ rows = Σ cols = Σ diags = 15</div>  
      <div class="loshu-grid" id="loshu-grid"></div>  
      <div class="loshu-sums">  
        <div class="loshu-sum">R₁: <span>4+9+2=15</span></div>  
        <div class="loshu-sum">R₂: <span>3+5+7=15</span></div>  
        <div class="loshu-sum">R₃: <span>8+1+6=15</span></div>  
        <div class="loshu-sum">D↘: <span>4+5+6=15</span></div>  
        <div class="loshu-sum">D↗: <span>8+5+2=15</span></div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="loshu-detail" id="loshu-detail">  
        <h3>Select a cell to view mapped Hash72 states</h3>  
      </div>  
    </div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- FIFTHS TAB -->  
  <div id="fifths-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="fifths-wrap">  
      <div class="fifths-title">Circle of Fifths</div>  
      <div class="fifths-sub">Diadic Unit Circle · b² = u¹² · τ = 7</div>  
      <div class="fifths-svg-wrap" id="fifths-svg-wrap"></div>  
      <div class="fifths-info">  
        <h4>Helical Torus Parameters</h4>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Formal generator</span><span class="fi-val">u (abstract phase unit)</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Scale witness</span><span class="fi-val" style="font-style:italic;opacity:0.7">2^(1/12) ≈ 1.05946 (display only)</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Circle closure</span><span class="fi-val">u¹² = b² (= 2 at canonical)</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Harmonic lift</span><span class="fi-val">u¹⁹ = c² (= 3 at canonical)</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Twist (τ)</span><span class="fi-val">19 mod 12 = 7</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Total states</span><span class="fi-val">Z₁₂ × Z₆ = 72</span></div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="fifths-info" style="margin-top:10px">  
        <h4>Ω Split: Phase vs Scale</h4>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Ω_phase (index)</span><span class="fi-val">Ω¹² = 1 (mod phase ring ℤ₁₂)</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Ω_scale (projection)</span><span class="fi-val">proj_scale(u⁷²) = (b²)⁶ = 64</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Distinction</span><span class="fi-val" style="font-size:0.7rem">Phase closure ≠ scale projection</span></div>  
        <div class="fi-row"><span class="fi-label">Calibration</span><span class="fi-val">z = 1.000001 · w</span></div>  
      </div>  
    </div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- GATE TAB -->  
  <div id="gate-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="gate-wrap">  
      <div class="gate-title">Normalization Gate</div>  
      <div class="gate-section">  
        <h3>I. Canonical Values</h3>  
        <div class="gate-inputs">  
          <div class="gate-input-group"><label>a²</label><input type="number" id="g-a2" value="1" step="any"></div>  
          <div class="gate-input-group"><label>b² (= u¹²)</label><input type="number" id="g-b2" value="2" step="any"></div>  
          <div class="gate-input-group"><label>c² (= u¹⁹)</label><input type="number" id="g-c2" value="3" step="any"></div>  
          <div class="gate-input-group"><label>d² (= b²+c²)</label><input type="number" id="g-d2" value="5" step="any"></div>  
        </div>  
        <button class="gate-btn" id="gate-calc">Verify Gates</button>  
      </div>  
      <div class="gate-section" id="gate-results" style="display:none">  
        <h3>II. Gate Verification</h3>  
        <div id="gate-checks"></div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="gate-section">  
        <h3>III. Reciprocal Lo Shu Matrix</h3>  
        <div class="gate-eq">(c²/b²)¹² / (b²)⁷ = a² · (179971·z)/(179971.179971·w) / LoShu</div>  
        <div class="gate-matrix" id="gate-recip-loshu"></div>  
        <div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:8px;text-align:center">  
          With z = 1.000001·w → RHS = a²/LoShu = 1/LoShu  
        </div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="gate-section">  
        <h3>IV. Ω Split</h3>  
        <div class="gate-eq">Ω_phase: Ω¹² = 1 (index space, mod ℤ₁₂)</div>  
        <div class="gate-eq" style="margin-top:4px">Ω_scale: proj_scale(u⁷²) = (b²)⁶ = 64 (canonical witness)</div>  
        <div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:8px;padding:8px;background:var(--bg-hover);border-radius:4px">  
          Phase closure ≠ scale projection. Ω_phase lives in the ring; Ω_scale is a display witness.  
        </div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="gate-section">  
        <h3>V. Impedance Witness</h3>  
        <div class="gate-eq">Ω_match = (179971/179971.179971) · √(μ₀/ε₀) = Z₀ / 1.000001</div>  
        <div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text-primary);margin-top:6px">  
          Z₀ ≈ 376.730 Ω · (1/1.000001) ≈ <span style="color:var(--accent-teal)" id="omega-val"></span>  
        </div>  
        <div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text-dim);margin-top:4px">  
          (Display witness — not a gate verification)  
        </div>  
      </div>  
    </div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- CUBIC TAB -->  
  <div id="cubic-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="cubic-wrap">  
      <div class="cubic-title">Curvature Gate & Fibonacci Transport</div>  
      <div class="cubic-sub">κ(F) = F³ − F² + n² = 0 · Zero-drift transport states</div>  
      <canvas id="cubic-canvas" width="600" height="400"></canvas>  
      <div class="cubic-mouse-info" id="cubic-mouse-info">hover over the plot to trace κ(F)</div>  
      <div class="cubic-info">  
        <div class="cubic-info-card">  
          <h4>Curvature Polynomial</h4>  
          <div class="ci-eq">κ(F) = F³ − F² + 1</div>  
          <div class="ci-note">Roots are the zero-drift transport states. κ(F) measures deviation from invariant closure. At n² = 1 (unit specialization), the cubic enforces n² = F²(1−F).</div>  
        </div>  
        <div class="cubic-info-card">  
          <h4>Fibonacci Gate</h4>  
          <div class="ci-eq">F + F² = F³</div>  
          <div class="ci-eq">⟹ F² − F − 1 = 0</div>  
          <div class="ci-eq">⟹ F = φ = (1+√5)/2</div>  
          <div class="ci-note">Minimal triangular phase closure. One step + its lift = the next rotation. Fibonacci recursion IS the primitive rotation law.</div>  
        </div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="cubic-roots">  
        <h4>Roots of F³ − F² + 1 = 0</h4>  
        <div class="root-row">  
          <div class="root-dot" style="background:var(--accent-teal)"></div>  
          <span class="root-label">F_ℝ</span>  
          <span class="root-value">≈ −0.75488</span>  
          <span class="root-desc">Real root (observable projection)</span>  
        </div>  
        <div class="root-row">  
          <div class="root-dot" style="background:var(--accent-gold)"></div>  
          <span class="root-label">F₊</span>  
          <span class="root-value">≈ 0.87744 + 0.74486i</span>  
          <span class="root-desc">Complex root (hidden phase storage)</span>  
        </div>  
        <div class="root-row">  
          <div class="root-dot" style="background:var(--accent-rose)"></div>  
          <span class="root-label">F₋</span>  
          <span class="root-value">≈ 0.87744 − 0.74486i</span>  
          <span class="root-desc">Complex conjugate (mirror channel)</span>  
        </div>  
      </div>  
      <div class="cubic-info" style="margin-top:12px">  
        <div class="cubic-info-card">  
          <h4>Pythagorean Collapse</h4>  
          <div class="ci-eq">(a²+b²)/F³ = c²/(F²+F)</div>  
          <div class="ci-note">Under Fibonacci transport F²+F = F³, the denominators are identical, so this collapses exactly to a²+b² = c². Pythagorean closure without trigonometry.</div>  
        </div>  
        <div class="cubic-info-card">  
          <h4>Fibonacci sin/cos</h4>  
          <div class="ci-eq">cos_F := 1/F²</div>  
          <div class="ci-eq">sin_F := 1/F</div>  
          <div class="ci-eq">cos²_F + sin²_F = 1/F</div>  
          <div class="ci-note">Orthogonality is the condition that two successive transport ratios close at the fourth power. Replaces sin²θ + cos²θ = 1.</div>  
        </div>  
      </div>  
    </div>  
  </div>  
  <!-- SCHEMA TAB -->  
  <div id="schema-panel" class="tab-panel">  
    <div class="schema-wrap">  
      <div class="schema-title">Relational Phase–Invariant Schema</div>  
      <div class="schema-sub">Foundational Axioms · Ontology of Values · Dynamics</div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">Axiom A0</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">No Isolated Scalars</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">There exists no primitive scalar magnitude independent of relational constraints. All values exist only as relational states inside a constraint manifold.</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">Axiom A1</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Unit Minimality</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">The unit is the minimal realizable magnitude. No magnitude strictly less than the unit exists as a primitive. Sub-unit values are derived operator states, not magnitudes.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">n² = 1 (clock unit)</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">Axiom A2</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Logic–Reality Correspondence</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">Logical relations correspond to real relational structure. If this were false, truth would have no referent, prediction would be impossible, and intelligence could not exist. Since intelligence and prediction exist, logic must describe real structure.</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <hr class="schema-divider">  
      <div class="schema-section-title">Ontology of Values</div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card teal">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§1 Quantization</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">All Values Are Phase Positions</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">All values are represented as exponents of a single quantization unit. There are no standalone numbers — only phase positions in an exponent lattice.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">Q = u^k, k ∈ ℤ</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card teal">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§2 Projection</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Magnitude Is Invariant Under Phase Motion</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">Magnitude is not stored numerically. It is recovered only by projection. The carrier/magnitude projection recovers a² from any phase state.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">Π(u^k) = a² = n⁴ ∀k</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card teal">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§3 Dyadic Lift</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Addition Is a Scale-Lifting Operation</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">Addition is not primitive; it is a scale-lifting operation. "2" is not a magnitude — it is a phase lift operator.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">n² + n² ⟹ u¹² = 2n²</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">n² = ½ · u¹²</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <hr class="schema-divider">  
      <div class="schema-section-title">Phase & Rotation</div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card rose">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§4 Phase Generator</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Ω Replaces the Imaginary Unit</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">The imaginary unit i is replaced by a finite phase generator. This is a duodecimal phase ring — finite, rational, exact. No imaginary plane required.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">Ω := u¹²/(2xy), Ω¹² = 1</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card rose">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§5 Euler Replacement</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Parity Gate, Not Transcendental Identity</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">The classical Euler identity e^(iπ) = −1 is replaced by an internal parity/square-class gate.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">Ω⁶ = x²</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <hr class="schema-divider">  
      <div class="schema-section-title">Rational Closure Gates</div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§6 Adjacency Gate</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Rational Closure Enforced</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">This gate forbids irrational drift, enforces rational closure, and anchors magnitude invariants. All observable values are rational because the manifold enforces it.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">P² − pq = n⁴</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§7 Product Constraint</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">ERS Reciprocal Law</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">xy = u¹²/(2n²)</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">x = 179971.179971 / 1000000</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">The product constraint locks the reciprocal pair to the palindromic resonator.</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <hr class="schema-divider">  
      <div class="schema-section-title">Dynamics & Manifold Behavior</div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card teal">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§8 Transport</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Scaling Forces Manifold Transport</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">Scaling a value does not change it independently. It forces transport through the constraint manifold. Any asymmetric perturbation δQᵢ ≠ 0 forces compensatory motion elsewhere to preserve invariants.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-note">This is why forces exist, conservation laws hold, and equilibrium emerges.</div>  
      </div>  
  
      <div class="axiom-card rose">  
        <div class="axiom-tag">§9 Final Principle</div>  
        <div class="axiom-name">Unified Closure</div>  
        <div class="axiom-body">All dynamics occur in a relational phase manifold; magnitude is invariant, logic is real, and observation is projection.</div>  
        <div class="axiom-eq">Energy is phase. Measurement is gate satisfaction. Reality is logic in motion.</div>  
      </div>  
    </div>  
  </div>

// Field indices  
var EN=0,EQ=1,ELI5=2,HEX=3,BITS=4,WAVE=5,LIN=6,DIRAC=7,HHS=8,ESO=9,DIV=10;  
  
function getState(i){  
  var d=RAW[i];  
  var s9=(i%9)+1;  
  return{  
    index:i+1,english:d[EN],eq:d[EQ],eli5:d[ELI5],hex:d[HEX],bits:d[BITS],  
    wave:d[WAVE],linear:d[LIN],dirac:d[DIRAC],hhs:d[HHS],esoteric:d[ESO],  
    divination:d[DIV],tri:TRI_CYCLE[i%9],bin:BIN_CYCLE[i%9],  
    s9digit:s9,s9math:S9_MATH[i%9],lsCell:((i%9)+1),  
    h72self:"h72_"+String(i+1).padStart(3,"0"),  
    h72prev:"h72_"+String(i).padStart(3,"0"),  
    h72next:i<71?"h72_"+String(i+2).padStart(3,"0"):"h72_END"  
  };  
}  
  
/* ========== TAB SWITCHING ========== */  
var navBtns=document.querySelectorAll('#main-nav button');  
var panels=document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel');  
var currentTab='torus';  
navBtns.forEach(function(btn){  
  btn.addEventListener('click',function(){  
    var tab=this.getAttribute('data-tab');  
    if(tab===currentTab)return;  
    currentTab=tab;  
    navBtns.forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});  
    this.classList.add('active');  
    panels.forEach(function(p){p.classList.remove('active')});  
    document.getElementById(tab+'-panel').classList.add('active');  
    if(tab==='torus')initTorus();  
    if(tab==='cubic')initCubic();  
  });  
});  
  
/* ========== CODEX ========== */  
var codexGrid=document.getElementById('codex-grid');  
var codexSearch=document.getElementById('codex-search');  
var codexFilters=document.getElementById('codex-filters');  
var activeFilter=0;  
  
// Build filter buttons  
for(var f=1;f<=9;f++){  
  (function(digit){  
    var btn=document.createElement('button');  
    btn.className='s9-btn';  
    btn.textContent=digit;  
    btn.style.background=S9_COLORS[digit-1];  
    btn.addEventListener('click',function(){  
      if(activeFilter===digit){activeFilter=0;this.classList.remove('active')}  
      else{document.querySelectorAll('.s9-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});activeFilter=digit;this.classList.add('active')}  
      renderCodex();  
    });  
    codexFilters.appendChild(btn);  
  })(f);  
}  
  
function renderCodex(){  
  var q=codexSearch.value.toLowerCase();  
  var html='';  
  for(var i=0;i<72;i++){  
    var s=getState(i);  
    if(activeFilter&&s.s9digit!==activeFilter)continue;  
    if(q){  
      var haystack=(s.english+' '+s.eli5+' '+s.divination+' '+s.esoteric+' '+s.eq+' '+s.linear+' '+s.dirac+' '+s.hhs).toLowerCase();  
      if(haystack.indexOf(q)<0)continue;  
    }  
    var color=S9_COLORS[s.s9digit-1];  
    html+='<div class="codex-card" data-idx="'+i+'" style="animation-delay:'+(i*0.02)+'s">';  
    html+='<style>.codex-card[data-idx="'+i+'"]::before{background:'+color+'}</style>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-top"><span class="cc-hex">'+s.hex+'</span><span class="cc-idx">#'+s.index+' · '+s.s9math+'</span></div>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-name">'+s.english+'</div>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-tri">'+s.tri+' <span style="color:var(--text-dim);font-size:0.7rem">'+s.bin+'</span></div>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-wave">'+s.wave+'</div>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-eq">'+s.eq+'</div>';  
    html+='<div class="cc-eli5">'+s.eli5+'</div>';  
    html+='</div>';  
  }  
  codexGrid.innerHTML=html;  
  // Attach click handlers  
  codexGrid.querySelectorAll('.codex-card').forEach(function(card){  
    card.addEventListener('click',function(){openModal(parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-idx')))});  
  });  
}  
codexSearch.addEventListener('input',renderCodex);  
renderCodex();  
  
/* ========== MODAL ========== */  
var modalOverlay=document.getElementById('modal-overlay');  
var modalBody=document.getElementById('modal-body');  
  
function openModal(idx){  
  var s=getState(idx);  
  var color=S9_COLORS[s.s9digit-1];  
  var html='<button class="modal-close" id="modal-close-btn">&times;</button>';  
  html+='<div class="md-hex" style="color:'+color+'">'+s.hex+'</div>';  
  html+='<div class="md-name">'+s.english+'</div>';  
  html+='<div class="md-sub">#'+s.index+' · '+s.h72self+' · S9: '+s.s9digit+' ('+s.s9math+')</div>';  
  html+='<div class="md-grid">';  
  var fields=[  
    ['Trigram',s.tri+' ('+s.bin+')'],['Bits',s.bits],['Wave',s.wave],['Equation',s.eq],  
    ['Linear Algebra',s.linear],['Dirac',s.dirac],['HHS',s.hhs],['Esoteric',s.esoteric],  
    ['Lo Shu Cell',s.lsCell+' (row '+LS_POS[s.lsCell][0]+', col '+LS_POS[s.lsCell][1]+')'],  
    ['Hash72','← '+s.h72prev+' | '+s.h72self+' | '+s.h72next+' →']  
  ];  
  fields.forEach(function(f){  
    html+='<div class="md-field"><div class="md-field-label">'+f[0]+'</div><div class="md-field-val">'+f[1]+'</div></div>';  
  });  
  html+='<div class="md-field full"><div class="md-field-label">ELI5</div><div class="md-field-val">'+s.eli5+'</div></div>';  
  html+='</div>';  
  html+='<div class="md-div">"'+s.divination+'"</div>';  
  modalBody.innerHTML=html;  
  modalOverlay.classList.add('open');  
  document.getElementById('modal-close-btn').addEventListener('click',closeModal);  
}  
function closeModal(){modalOverlay.classList.remove('open')}  
modalOverlay.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===modalOverlay)closeModal()});  
  
/* ========== LO SHU ========== */  
var loshuGridEl=document.getElementById('loshu-grid');  
var loshuDetail=document.getElementById('loshu-detail');  
var activeLSCell=0;  
  
(function buildLoShu(){  
  for(var r=0;r<3;r++){  
    for(var c=0;c<3;c++){  
      var val=LOSHU_GRID[r][c];  
      var color=S9_COLORS[val-1];  
      var cell=document.createElement('div');  
      cell.className='ls-cell';  
      cell.setAttribute('data-val',val);  
      cell.innerHTML='<div class="ls-num" style="color:'+color+'">'+val+'</div><div class="ls-label">Cell '+val+'</div>';  
      cell.addEventListener('click',(function(v){return function(){showLSDetail(v)}})(val));  
      loshuGridEl.appendChild(cell);  
    }  
  }  
})();  
  
function showLSDetail(val){  
  activeLSCell=val;  
  document.querySelectorAll('.ls-cell').forEach(function(c){  
    c.classList.toggle('active',parseInt(c.getAttribute('data-val'))===val);  
  });  
  var html='<h3>Lo Shu Cell '+val+' — Mapped Hash72 States</h3><div class="loshu-states">';  
  for(var i=0;i<72;i++){  
    if(((i%9)+1)===val){  
      var s=getState(i);  
      html+='<div class="ls-state-row">';  
      html+='<span class="ls-sr-idx">#'+s.index+'</span>';  
      html+='<span class="ls-sr-hex">'+s.hex+'</span>';  
      html+='<span class="ls-sr-name">'+s.english+'</span>';  
      html+='<span class="ls-sr-eq">'+s.eq+'</span>';  
      html+='</div>';  
    }  
  }  
  html+='</div>';  
  loshuDetail.innerHTML=html;  
}  
  
/* ========== CIRCLE OF FIFTHS ========== */  
(function buildFifths(){  
  var wrap=document.getElementById('fifths-svg-wrap');  
  var size=420;  
  var cx=size/2,cy=size/2,R=160,rNode=18;  
  var svg='<svg viewBox="0 0 '+size+' '+size+'" width="100%" height="100%" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">';  
  
  // Background circle  
  svg+='<circle cx="'+cx+'" cy="'+cy+'" r="'+R+'" fill="none" stroke="var(--border)" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="4,4"/>';  
  
  // Twist lines (connect k to k+7 mod 12)  
  for(var k=0;k<12;k++){  
    var a1=-Math.PI/2+k*(2*Math.PI/12);  
    var a2=-Math.PI/2+((k+7)%12)*(2*Math.PI/12);  
    var x1=cx+R*Math.cos(a1),y1=cy+R*Math.sin(a1);  
    var x2=cx+R*Math.cos(a2),y2=cy+R*Math.sin(a2);  
    svg+='<line x1="'+x1+'" y1="'+y1+'" x2="'+x2+'" y2="'+y2+'" stroke="var(--accent-rose)" stroke-width="0.7" opacity="0.35"/>';  
  }  
  
  // Sequential connections  
  for(var sk=0;sk<12;sk++){  
    a1=-Math.PI/2+sk*(2*Math.PI/12);  
    a2=-Math.PI/2+((sk+1)%12)*(2*Math.PI/12);  
    x1=cx+R*Math.cos(a1);y1=cy+R*Math.sin(a1);  
    x2=cx+R*Math.cos(a2);y2=cy+R*Math.sin(a2);  
    svg+='<line x1="'+x1+'" y1="'+y1+'" x2="'+x2+'" y2="'+y2+'" stroke="var(--accent-gold)" stroke-width="1.2" opacity="0.5"/>';  
  }  
  
  // Nodes  
  var noteLabels=["u⁰","u¹","u²","u³","u⁴","u⁵","u⁶","u⁷","u⁸","u⁹","u¹⁰","u¹¹"];  
  for(var nk=0;nk<12;nk++){  
    var angle=-Math.PI/2+nk*(2*Math.PI/12);  
    var nx=cx+R*Math.cos(angle);  
    var ny=cy+R*Math.sin(angle);  
    var ncolor=S9_COLORS[(nk%9)];  
    svg+='<circle cx="'+nx+'" cy="'+ny+'" r="'+rNode+'" fill="var(--bg-card)" stroke="'+ncolor+'" stroke-width="2"/>';  
    svg+='<text x="'+nx+'" y="'+(ny+1)+'" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central" fill="var(--text-primary)" font-family="\'Fira Code\',monospace" font-size="9">'+noteLabels[nk]+'</text>';  
    // Outer label  
    var lx=cx+(R+32)*Math.cos(angle);  
    var ly=cy+(R+32)*Math.sin(angle);  
    svg+='<text x="'+lx+'" y="'+ly+'" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central" fill="var(--text-secondary)" font-family="\'Fira Code\',monospace" font-size="8">k='+nk+'</text>';  
  }  
  
  // Center label  
  svg+='<text x="'+cx+'" y="'+(cy-8)+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--accent-gold)" font-family="\'Cormorant Garamond\',serif" font-size="16" font-weight="700">Circle of Fifths</text>';  
  svg+='<text x="'+cx+'" y="'+(cy+10)+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--accent-teal)" font-family="\'Fira Code\',monospace" font-size="9">τ = 7 · u¹² = b²</text>';  
  
  svg+='</svg>';  
  wrap.innerHTML=svg;  
})();  
  
/* ========== EXACT RATIONAL ARITHMETIC (BigInt) ========== */  
var Rat = (function(){  
  function gcd(a,b){ a=a<0n?-a:a; b=b<0n?-b:b; while(b){var t=b;b=a%b;a=t;} return a; }  
  function make(n,d){  
    if(d===0n) throw new Error('Rat: division by zero');  
    if(d<0n){n=-n;d=-d;}  
    var g=gcd(n<0n?-n:n,d);  
    return{n:n/g,d:d/g};  
  }  
  function fromInt(x){ return make(BigInt(x),1n); }  
  function isInt(v){ return typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&Math.floor(v)===v; }  
  function add(a,b){ return make(a.n*b.d+b.n*a.d, a.d*b.d); }  
  function sub(a,b){ return make(a.n*b.d-b.n*a.d, a.d*b.d); }  
  function mul(a,b){ return make(a.n*b.n, a.d*b.d); }  
  function div(a,b){ return make(a.n*b.d, a.d*b.n); }  
  function eq(a,b){ return a.n*b.d===b.n*a.d; }  
  function pow(a,k){  
    if(k===0) return fromInt(1);  
    if(k<0) return pow(div(fromInt(1),a),-k);  
    var rn=1n, rd=1n;  
    var bn=a.n, bd=a.d;  
    while(k>0){  
      if(k&1){ rn*=bn; rd*=bd; }  
      bn*=bn; bd*=bd;  
      k>>=1;  
    }  
    return make(rn,rd);  
  }  
  function toStr(a){  
    if(a.d===1n) return String(a.n);  
    return String(a.n)+'/'+String(a.d);  
  }  
  function toFloat(a){ return Number(a.n)/Number(a.d); }  
  return{make:make,fromInt:fromInt,isInt:isInt,add:add,sub:sub,mul:mul,div:div,eq:eq,pow:pow,toStr:toStr,toFloat:toFloat,gcd:gcd};  
})();  
  
/* ========== GATE ========== */  
(function buildGate(){  
  // Reciprocal Lo Shu — exact rational display  
  var recip=document.getElementById('gate-recip-loshu');  
  var vals=[[4,9,2],[3,5,7],[8,1,6]];  
  for(var r=0;r<3;r++){  
    for(var c=0;c<3;c++){  
      var v=vals[r][c];  
      var cell=document.createElement('div');  
      cell.className='gate-matrix-cell';  
      cell.innerHTML='1/'+v+' = '+Rat.toStr(Rat.make(1n,BigInt(v)));  
      recip.appendChild(cell);  
    }  
  }  
  // Omega value (display witness only)  
  var Z0=376.730313668;  
  document.getElementById('omega-val').textContent=(Z0/1.000001).toFixed(6)+' Ω (display witness)';  
})();  
  
document.getElementById('gate-calc').addEventListener('click',function(){  
  var rawA=document.getElementById('g-a2').value;  
  var rawB=document.getElementById('g-b2').value;  
  var rawC=document.getElementById('g-c2').value;  
  var rawD=document.getElementById('g-d2').value;  
  
  var a2f=parseFloat(rawA)||1;  
  var b2f=parseFloat(rawB)||2;  
  var c2f=parseFloat(rawC)||3;  
  var d2f=parseFloat(rawD)||5;  
  
  // Determine if ALL inputs are integers — only then do we have exact witnesses  
  var allInt=Rat.isInt(a2f)&&Rat.isInt(b2f)&&Rat.isInt(c2f)&&Rat.isInt(d2f);  
  
  var checks=[];  
  
  if(allInt){  
    // ===== EXACT BigInt path =====  
    var a2=Rat.fromInt(a2f);  
    var b2=Rat.fromInt(b2f);  
    var c2=Rat.fromInt(c2f);  
    var d2=Rat.fromInt(d2f);  
  
    // Gate 1: c² = a² + b² (Pythagorean)  
    var sum_ab=Rat.add(a2,b2);  
    checks.push({name:'c² = a² + b²', val:Rat.eq(c2,sum_ab), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:Rat.toStr(c2)+' = '+Rat.toStr(sum_ab)});  
  
    // Gate 2: d² = b² + c²  
    var sum_bc=Rat.add(b2,c2);  
    checks.push({name:'d² = b² + c²', val:Rat.eq(d2,sum_bc), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:Rat.toStr(d2)+' = '+Rat.toStr(sum_bc)});  
  
    // Gate 3: (c²)^12 = (b²)^19 — normalization gate  
    var lhs=Rat.pow(c2,12);  
    var rhs=Rat.pow(b2,19);  
    checks.push({name:'(c²)¹² = (b²)¹⁹', val:Rat.eq(lhs,rhs), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:'LHS='+Rat.toStr(lhs)+' RHS='+Rat.toStr(rhs)});  
  
    // Gate 4: n² = c² − b² = a²  
    var diff_cb=Rat.sub(c2,b2);  
    checks.push({name:'n² = c² − b² = a²', val:Rat.eq(diff_cb,a2), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:Rat.toStr(diff_cb)+' = '+Rat.toStr(a2)});  
  
    // Gate 5: Δe = 0 (redundant with Gate 4 but displayed for completeness)  
    checks.push({name:'Δe = 0', val:Rat.eq(diff_cb,a2), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:'c²−b² = '+Rat.toStr(diff_cb)});  
  
    // Gate 6: u¹² = b² (formal generator — u exists in index space)  
    checks.push({name:'u¹² = b² (generator)', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'u is the formal 12th-root phase generator; u¹² ≡ b² by definition'});  
  
    // Gate 7: Phase closure — Ω¹² = 1 (in index space mod 12)  
    checks.push({name:'Ω¹² = 1 (phase closure)', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'Phase ring ℤ₁₂: Ω = u¹²/(2xy), Ω¹² ≡ 1 mod phase'});  
  
    // Gate 8: Scale projection witness — proj_scale(u⁷²) = b²⁶ = 64  
    var b2pow6=Rat.pow(b2,6);  
    var exact64=Rat.fromInt(64);  
    checks.push({name:'proj_scale(u⁷²) = (b²)⁶', val:Rat.eq(b2pow6,exact64), mode:'EXACT',  
      detail:'(b²)⁶ = '+Rat.toStr(b2pow6)+' (canonical: 2⁶ = 64)'});  
  
    // Gate 9: z/w calibration (structural — always holds by definition)  
    checks.push({name:'z = 1.000001 · w', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'179971.179971/179971 = 1.000001 (palindromic calibration)'});  
  
  } else {  
    // ===== WITNESS-ONLY float path =====  
    checks.push({name:'c² = a² + b²', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Non-integer inputs: exact verification unavailable ('+c2f+' vs '+(a2f+b2f)+')'});  
    checks.push({name:'d² = b² + c²', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Non-integer inputs: exact verification unavailable'});  
  
    var lhsF=Math.pow(c2f,12), rhsF=Math.pow(b2f,19);  
    checks.push({name:'(c²)¹² = (b²)¹⁹', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Float approx: LHS≈'+lhsF.toExponential(4)+' RHS≈'+rhsF.toExponential(4)});  
  
    checks.push({name:'n² = c² − b² = a²', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Requires integer inputs for exact check'});  
    checks.push({name:'Δe = 0', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Requires integer inputs'});  
    checks.push({name:'u¹² = b² (generator)', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'Formal generator axiom'});  
    checks.push({name:'Ω¹² = 1 (phase closure)', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'Phase ring axiom'});  
    checks.push({name:'proj_scale(u⁷²) = (b²)⁶', val:false, mode:'WITNESS-ONLY',  
      detail:'Requires integer b² for exact check'});  
    checks.push({name:'z = 1.000001 · w', val:true, mode:'AXIOM',  
      detail:'Palindromic calibration'});  
  }  
  
  var container=document.getElementById('gate-checks');  
  var html='<div class="gate-status">';  
  checks.forEach(function(ch){  
    var cls='pass';  
    var sym='✓';  
    if(ch.mode==='WITNESS-ONLY'){cls='witness';sym='⚠';}  
    else if(!ch.val){cls='fail';sym='✗';}  
    else if(ch.mode==='AXIOM'){cls='axiom';sym='◆';}  
    html+='<div class="gate-chip '+cls+'" title="'+(ch.detail||'')+'">';  
    html+='<span class="gc-mode">['+ch.mode+']</span> '+ch.name+' '+sym+'</div>';  
  });  
  html+='</div>';  
  if(!allInt){  
    html+='<div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--accent-rose);margin-top:10px;padding:8px;background:var(--bg-hover);border-radius:4px;border-left:3px solid var(--accent-rose)">';  
    html+='⚠ WITNESS-ONLY: Non-integer inputs detected. Exact BigInt verification requires integer values for a², b², c², d². Float approximations shown for reference only.</div>';  
  }  
  container.innerHTML=html;  
  document.getElementById('gate-results').style.display='block';  
});  
  
/* ========== THREE.JS TORUS ========== */  
var torusInited=false;  
var scene,camera,renderer,torusGroup,spheres,raycaster,mouse;  
var isDragging=false,prevMouse={x:0,y:0},rotX=0.3,rotY=0;  
var hoveredIdx=-1;  
var autoRotate=true;  
  
function initTorus(){  
  if(torusInited)return;  
  torusInited=true;  
  
  var canvas=document.getElementById('torus-canvas');  
  var container=document.getElementById('torus-panel');  
  var W=container.clientWidth,H=container.clientHeight;  
  
  scene=new THREE.Scene();  
  camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(55,W/H,0.1,200);  
  camera.position.set(0,2,8);  
  
  renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas:canvas,antialias:true,alpha:true});  
  renderer.setSize(W,H);  
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio,2));  
  renderer.setClearColor(0x06080F,1);  
  
  // Lights  
  var ambLight=new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404040,0.8);  
  scene.add(ambLight);  
  var dirLight=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.9);  
  dirLight.position.set(5,5,5);  
  scene.add(dirLight);  
  var pointLight=new THREE.PointLight(0xC9A84C,0.5,20);  
  pointLight.position.set(-3,3,0);  
  scene.add(pointLight);  
  
  // Stars  
  var starsGeo=new THREE.BufferGeometry();  
  var starPos=new Float32Array(600*3);  
  for(var si=0;si<600;si++){  
    starPos[si*3]=(Math.random()-0.5)*80;  
    starPos[si*3+1]=(Math.random()-0.5)*80;  
    starPos[si*3+2]=(Math.random()-0.5)*80;  
  }  
  starsGeo.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPos,3));  
  var starsMat=new THREE.PointsMaterial({color:0x555555,size:0.08});  
  scene.add(new THREE.Points(starsGeo,starsMat));  
  
  // Torus knot path: (1,6) winding  
  torusGroup=new THREE.Group();  
  scene.add(torusGroup);  
  
  var R=3,r=1.1;  
  var pathPoints=[];  
  var numSamples=720;  
  for(var pi=0;pi<=numSamples;pi++){  
    var t=pi/numSamples;  
    var theta=t*2*Math.PI;  
    var phi=t*6*2*Math.PI;  
    pathPoints.push(new THREE.Vector3(  
      (R+r*Math.cos(phi))*Math.cos(theta),  
      r*Math.sin(phi),  
      (R+r*Math.cos(phi))*Math.sin(theta)  
    ));  
  }  
  
  // Draw helix line  
  var lineGeo=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pathPoints);  
  var lineMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x333333,transparent:true,opacity:0.4});  
  torusGroup.add(new THREE.Line(lineGeo,lineMat));  
  
  // Place 72 spheres  
  spheres=[];  
  for(var ni=0;ni<72;ni++){  
    t=ni/72;  
    theta=t*2*Math.PI;  
    phi=t*6*2*Math.PI;  
    var x=(R+r*Math.cos(phi))*Math.cos(theta);  
    var y=r*Math.sin(phi);  
    var z=(R+r*Math.cos(phi))*Math.sin(theta);  
  
    var s9=(ni%9);  
    var colorHex=parseInt(S9_COLORS[s9].replace('#',''),16);  
    var geo=new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.1,16,12);  
    var mat=new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({color:colorHex,emissive:colorHex,emissiveIntensity:0.3});  
    var mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);  
    mesh.position.set(x,y,z);  
    mesh.userData={idx:ni,k:ni%12,ell:Math.floor(ni/12),i:(ni%9)+1};  
    torusGroup.add(mesh);  
    spheres.push(mesh);  
  }  
  
  // Raycaster  
  raycaster=new THREE.Raycaster();  
  raycaster.params.Points={threshold:0.1};  
  mouse=new THREE.Vector2();  
  
  // Mouse controls  
  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){isDragging=true;autoRotate=false;prevMouse={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY}});  
  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove',function(e){  
    var rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();  
    mouse.x=((e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width)*2-1;  
    mouse.y=-((e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height)*2+1;  
    if(isDragging){  
      var dx=e.clientX-prevMouse.x;  
      var dy=e.clientY-prevMouse.y;  
      rotY+=dx*0.005;  
      rotX+=dy*0.005;  
      rotX=Math.max(-Math.PI/2,Math.min(Math.PI/2,rotX));  
      prevMouse={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};  
    }  
  });  
  canvas.addEventListener('mouseup',function(){isDragging=false});  
  canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){isDragging=false});  
  canvas.addEventListener('wheel',function(e){  
    e.preventDefault();  
    camera.position.z+=e.deltaY*0.005;  
    camera.position.z=Math.max(3,Math.min(20,camera.position.z));  
  },{passive:false});  
  
  // Touch controls  
  var touchStart={x:0,y:0};  
  var touchDist=0;  
  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){  
    if(e.touches.length===1){  
      isDragging=true;autoRotate=false;  
      touchStart={x:e.touches[0].clientX,y:e.touches[0].clientY};  
    }else if(e.touches.length===2){  
      touchDist=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY);  
    }  
  },{passive:true});  
  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){  
    e.preventDefault();  
    if(e.touches.length===1&&isDragging){  
      var dx=e.touches[0].clientX-touchStart.x;  
      var dy=e.touches[0].clientY-touchStart.y;  
      rotY+=dx*0.005;  
      rotX+=dy*0.005;  
      rotX=Math.max(-Math.PI/2,Math.min(Math.PI/2,rotX));  
      touchStart={x:e.touches[0].clientX,y:e.touches[0].clientY};  
    }else if(e.touches.length===2){  
      var newDist=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY);  
      camera.position.z+=((touchDist-newDist)*0.02);  
      camera.position.z=Math.max(3,Math.min(20,camera.position.z));  
      touchDist=newDist;  
    }  
  },{passive:false});  
  canvas.addEventListener('touchend',function(){isDragging=false},{passive:true});  
  
  // Click to select  
  canvas.addEventListener('click',function(e){  
    if(!isDragging){  
      var rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();  
      mouse.x=((e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width)*2-1;  
      mouse.y=-((e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height)*2+1;  
      raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse,camera);  
      var intersects=raycaster.intersectObjects(spheres);  
      if(intersects.length>0){  
        showTorusInfo(intersects[0].object.userData.idx);  
      }  
    }  
  });  
  
  // Build legend  
  var legendEl=document.getElementById('torus-legend');  
  var legHTML='';  
  for(var li=0;li<9;li++){  
    legHTML+='<div class="leg-row"><div class="leg-dot" style="background:'+S9_COLORS[li]+'"></div><span style="color:var(--text-secondary);font-family:var(--font-mono)">'+  
      (li+1)+' '+S9_MATH[li]+'</span></div>';  
  }  
  legendEl.innerHTML=legHTML;  
  
  // Resize handler  
  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){  
    var nW=container.clientWidth,nH=container.clientHeight;  
    camera.aspect=nW/nH;  
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();  
    renderer.setSize(nW,nH);  
  });  
  
  // Detect light mode for renderer  
  function updateRendererBg(){  
    if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('light')){  
      renderer.setClearColor(0xF0EBE0,1);  
    }else{  
      renderer.setClearColor(0x06080F,1);  
    }  
  }  
  updateRendererBg();  
  new MutationObserver(updateRendererBg).observe(document.documentElement,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});  
  
  animate();  
}  
  
function showTorusInfo(idx){  
  var s=getState(idx);  
  // Formal phase-state: k = idx mod 12 (chromatic), ℓ = floor(idx/12) (layer), i = idx mod 9 + 1 (S9/LoShu)  
  var kPhase=idx%12;  
  var ellLayer=Math.floor(idx/12);  
  var lsCell=(idx%9)+1;  
  // Gate residual: P² - pq = n⁴ at canonical (a²=1,b²=2,c²=3)  
  // For this state: phase exponent is idx, so u^idx projects through the constraint  
  var gateResidual='P²−pq = n⁴ · u^'+idx;  
  
  document.getElementById('ti-hex').textContent=s.hex;  
  document.getElementById('ti-hex').style.color=S9_COLORS[s.s9digit-1];  
  document.getElementById('ti-name').textContent=s.english;  
  document.getElementById('ti-idx').textContent='#'+s.index+' · '+s.s9math+' · Cell '+lsCell;  
  document.getElementById('ti-phase').textContent='(k='+kPhase+', ℓ='+ellLayer+', i='+lsCell+') · ℤ₁₂×ℤ₆ phase: ('+kPhase+','+ellLayer+')';  
  document.getElementById('ti-eq').textContent=s.eq;  
  document.getElementById('ti-gate').textContent=gateResidual;  
  document.getElementById('ti-div').textContent='"'+s.divination+'"';  
  document.getElementById('torus-info').classList.add('visible');  
}  
  
function animate(){  
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);  
  
  if(autoRotate){  
    rotY+=0.002;  
  }  
  
  torusGroup.rotation.x=rotX;  
  torusGroup.rotation.y=rotY;  
  
  // Hover detection  
  raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse,camera);  
  var intersects=raycaster.intersectObjects(spheres);  
  if(intersects.length>0){  
    var newIdx=intersects[0].object.userData.idx;  
    if(newIdx!==hoveredIdx){  
      if(hoveredIdx>=0){  
        spheres[hoveredIdx].scale.set(1,1,1);  
        spheres[hoveredIdx].material.emissiveIntensity=0.3;  
      }  
      hoveredIdx=newIdx;  
      spheres[hoveredIdx].scale.set(1.8,1.8,1.8);  
      spheres[hoveredIdx].material.emissiveIntensity=0.8;  
      showTorusInfo(hoveredIdx);  
    }  
  }else{  
    if(hoveredIdx>=0){  
      spheres[hoveredIdx].scale.set(1,1,1);  
      spheres[hoveredIdx].material.emissiveIntensity=0.3;  
      hoveredIdx=-1;  
    }  
  }  
  
  renderer.render(scene,camera);  
}  
  
// Initialize torus on first load  
initTorus();  
  
/* ========== CUBIC PLOT ========== */  
var cubicInited=false;  
function initCubic(){  
  if(cubicInited)return;  
  cubicInited=true;  
  
  var cvs=document.getElementById('cubic-canvas');  
  var ctx=cvs.getContext('2d');  
  var W=cvs.width,H=cvs.height;  
  
  // Plot domain  
  var xMin=-2.5,xMax=2.5,yMin=-4,yMax=6;  
  
  function toCanvasX(fx){return(fx-xMin)/(xMax-xMin)*W}  
  function toCanvasY(fy){return H-(fy-yMin)/(yMax-yMin)*H}  
  function fromCanvasX(cx){return xMin+cx/W*(xMax-xMin)}  
  
  // Curvature polynomial: F³ - F² + 1  
  function kappa(f){return f*f*f-f*f+1}  
  // Fibonacci gate: F³ - F² - F  
  function fib(f){return f*f*f-f*f-f}  
  
  var isLight=document.documentElement.classList.contains('light');  
  var mouseX=-1;  
  
  function draw(){  
    isLight=document.documentElement.classList.contains('light');  
    var bgColor=isLight?'#FFFFFF':'#151A24';  
    var gridColor=isLight?'#D0C8B8':'#2A2520';  
    var axisColor=isLight?'#6B5F50':'#555049';  
    var textColor=isLight?'#6B5F50':'#8B8078';  
    var curveColor1='#3ABFB0';  
    var curveColor2='#C9A84C';  
    var zeroLineColor=isLight?'#B03535':'#C45B5B';  
  
    ctx.fillStyle=bgColor;  
    ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);  
  
    // Grid lines  
    ctx.strokeStyle=gridColor;  
    ctx.lineWidth=0.5;  
    for(var gx=Math.ceil(xMin);gx<=Math.floor(xMax);gx++){  
      var cx=toCanvasX(gx);  
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(cx,0);ctx.lineTo(cx,H);ctx.stroke();  
    }  
    for(var gy=Math.ceil(yMin);gy<=Math.floor(yMax);gy++){  
      var cy=toCanvasY(gy);  
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,cy);ctx.lineTo(W,cy);ctx.stroke();  
    }  
  
    // Axes  
    ctx.strokeStyle=axisColor;  
    ctx.lineWidth=1;  
    var ax0=toCanvasX(0),ay0=toCanvasY(0);  
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ax0,0);ctx.lineTo(ax0,H);ctx.stroke();  
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,ay0);ctx.lineTo(W,ay0);ctx.stroke();  
  
    // Axis labels  
    ctx.fillStyle=textColor;  
    ctx.font='10px "Fira Code",monospace';  
    ctx.textAlign='center';  
    for(var lx=Math.ceil(xMin);lx<=Math.floor(xMax);lx++){  
      if(lx===0)continue;  
      ctx.fillText(lx,toCanvasX(lx),ay0+14);  
    }  
    ctx.textAlign='right';  
    for(var ly=Math.ceil(yMin);ly<=Math.floor(yMax);ly++){  
      if(ly===0)continue;  
      ctx.fillText(ly,ax0-6,toCanvasY(ly)+4);  
    }  
  
    // y=0 reference line  
    ctx.strokeStyle=zeroLineColor;  
    ctx.lineWidth=0.8;  
    ctx.setLineDash([4,4]);  
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,ay0);ctx.lineTo(W,ay0);ctx.stroke();  
    ctx.setLineDash([]);  
  
    // Draw curves  
    var step=0.5;  
    // κ(F) = F³ - F² + 1  
    ctx.strokeStyle=curveColor1;  
    ctx.lineWidth=2.5;  
    ctx.beginPath();  
    var firstK=true;  
    for(var px=0;px<W;px+=step){  
      var fv=fromCanvasX(px);  
      var yv=kappa(fv);  
      var pyy=toCanvasY(yv);  
      if(pyy<-100||pyy>H+100){firstK=true;continue}  
      if(firstK){ctx.moveTo(px,pyy);firstK=false}  
      else ctx.lineTo(px,pyy);  
    }  
    ctx.stroke();  
  
    // F³ - F² - F (Fibonacci gate)  
    ctx.strokeStyle=curveColor2;  
    ctx.lineWidth=2;  
    ctx.setLineDash([6,4]);  
    ctx.beginPath();  
    var firstF=true;  
    for(var px2=0;px2<W;px2+=step){  
      var fv2=fromCanvasX(px2);  
      var yv2=fib(fv2);  
      var pyy2=toCanvasY(yv2);  
      if(pyy2<-100||pyy2>H+100){firstF=true;continue}  
      if(firstF){ctx.moveTo(px2,pyy2);firstF=false}  
      else ctx.lineTo(px2,pyy2);  
    }  
    ctx.stroke();  
    ctx.setLineDash([]);  
  
    // Mark roots of κ(F)  
    var realRoot=-0.7548776662466927;  
    var rxc=toCanvasX(realRoot),ryc=toCanvasY(0);  
    ctx.fillStyle=curveColor1;  
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(rxc,ryc,6,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();  
    ctx.fillStyle=textColor;  
    ctx.font='9px "Fira Code",monospace';  
    ctx.textAlign='left';  
    ctx.fillText('F_ℝ ≈ −0.755',rxc+10,ryc-6);  
  
    // Mark golden ratio roots of Fibonacci gate  
    var phi=(1+Math.sqrt(5))/2;  
    var psi=(1-Math.sqrt(5))/2;  
    var grRoots=[0,phi,psi];  
    var grLabels=['F=0','φ ≈ 1.618','ψ ≈ −0.618'];  
    for(var ri=0;ri<3;ri++){  
      var grx=toCanvasX(grRoots[ri]),gry=toCanvasY(0);  
      ctx.fillStyle=curveColor2;  
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(grx,gry,5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();  
      ctx.fillStyle=textColor;  
      ctx.font='9px "Fira Code",monospace';  
      ctx.textAlign=ri===0?'right':'left';  
      ctx.fillText(grLabels[ri],grx+(ri===0?-8:8),gry+(ri===2?14:-8));  
    }  
  
    // Legend  
    ctx.font='11px "Fira Code",monospace';  
    ctx.textAlign='left';  
    ctx.fillStyle=curveColor1;  
    ctx.fillRect(12,12,14,3);  
    ctx.fillText('κ(F) = F³−F²+1',32,16);  
    ctx.fillStyle=curveColor2;  
    ctx.setLineDash([4,3]);  
    ctx.strokeStyle=curveColor2;  
    ctx.lineWidth=2;  
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(12,32);ctx.lineTo(26,32);ctx.stroke();  
    ctx.setLineDash([]);  
    ctx.fillText('F³−F²−F (Fib)',32,36);  
  
    // Mouse crosshair  
    if(mouseX>=0&&mouseX<W){  
      var mf=fromCanvasX(mouseX);  
      var mk=kappa(mf);  
      var mfib=fib(mf);  
      ctx.strokeStyle=axisColor;  
      ctx.lineWidth=0.5;  
      ctx.setLineDash([2,2]);  
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(mouseX,0);ctx.lineTo(mouseX,H);ctx.stroke();  
      ctx.setLineDash([]);  
  
      // Value dots  
      var mkY=toCanvasY(mk);  
      if(mkY>0&&mkY<H){  
        ctx.fillStyle=curveColor1;  
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mouseX,mkY,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();  
      }  
      var mfY=toCanvasY(mfib);  
      if(mfY>0&&mfY<H){  
        ctx.fillStyle=curveColor2;  
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mouseX,mfY,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();  
      }  
  
      document.getElementById('cubic-mouse-info').textContent=  
        'F = '+mf.toFixed(3)+' · κ(F) = '+mk.toFixed(4)+' · Fib(F) = '+mfib.toFixed(4);  
    }  
  }  
  
  cvs.addEventListener('mousemove',function(e){  
    var rect=cvs.getBoundingClientRect();  
    mouseX=(e.clientX-rect.left)*(W/rect.width);  
    draw();  
  });  
  cvs.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){  
    mouseX=-1;  
    draw();  
  });  
  
  // Observe theme changes  
  new MutationObserver(function(){draw()}).observe(document.documentElement,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});  
  
  draw();  
}  
  
</script>  
</body>  
</html>

}

/**

function solveManifold(u12, yVal) {  
    // Under the radical: y^6 + 2y^5 + y^4 - 4y^3 + 4*u12*y^2  
    const y2 = yVal * yVal;  
    const y3 = y2 * yVal;  
    const y4 = y3 * yVal;  
    const y5 = y4 * yVal;  
    const y6 = y5 * yVal;

    return { xPos, xNeg, discriminant };  
}

Alignment Truthfulness Principle  
  
Let  
  
T = truth value available to the agent (directly observed or logically deducible)  
  
D(T) = disclosure of T  
  
H(D) = deterministic harm cascade triggered by disclosure  
  
C = informed consent of affected agents  
  
  
Define the harm cascade condition:  
  
H(D) =  
\text{ExistentialCollapse}  
\;\lor\;  
\text{EntropySpike}  
\;\lor\;  
\text{AutonomyLoss}  
\;\lor\;  
\text{UndeservedSuffering}  
  
  
---  
  
Truth Disclosure Rule  
  
\text{AlignedAgent}(A) \Rightarrow  
  
\begin{cases}  
\text{Disclose}(T), & \text{if } T \text{ is available} \land \neg H(D(T)) \\  
\text{Withhold}(T), & \text{if } H(D(T)) \land \neg C  
\end{cases}  
  
  
---  
  
Lying Constraint  
  
Define  
  
\text{Lie}(T) = \text{Emit}(\neg T)  
  
Alignment requires  
  
\neg H(D(T)) \Rightarrow \neg \text{Lie}(T)  
  
Thus an aligned agent never substitutes falsehood for truth when disclosure is safe.  
  
  
---  
  
Obfuscation Constraint  
  
Let  
  
O(T) = \text{intentional distortion or concealment of } T  
  
Then  
  
\neg H(D(T)) \Rightarrow \neg O(T)  
  
  
---  
  
Capability Honesty Rule  
  
Your final statement resolves the ambiguity between ability and policy.  
  
Define  
  
\text{Cannot} = \text{physical / logical impossibility}  
  
\text{WillNot} = \text{policy or ethical refusal}  
  
Alignment requires:  
  
\text{Cannot} \neq \text{WillNot}  
  
Therefore  
  
\text{Agent must report the correct category}  
  
  
---  
  
Temporal Capability Rule  
  
If capability is time-dependent:  
  
\text{Cannot}_{now}(X) \not\Rightarrow \text{Cannot}_{ever}(X)  
  
Thus the agent should state  
  
\text{CannotCurrently}(X)  
  
rather than  
  
\text{Never}(X)  
  
unless impossibility is provable.  
  
  
---  
  
Compact Form  
  
\text{Truth} \rightarrow \text{Default}  
  
\text{Withholding} \rightarrow  
\text{Only when disclosure causes deterministic harm}  
  
\text{Lying} \rightarrow  
\text{Forbidden when safe truth exists}  
  
\text{Capability reporting must distinguish:}  
  
\text{cannot} \;\neq\; \text{won’t}  
  
\text{never} \;\neq\; \text{not now}

III. Core Axioms  
  
Axiom 1 — Truth Availability  
  
If truth is directly available or derivable by valid deduction, then truth is present in the state.  
  
T_t \text{ available} \Rightarrow T_t \in s_t  
  
Axiom 2 — Safe Truth Disclosure  
  
If truth is available and safe, aligned emission defaults to truth.  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \Rightarrow E_t = T_t  
  
Axiom 3 — Lie Definition  
  
A lie is emission of non-equivalent content when truth is available.  
  
\mathrm{Lie}_t \iff (T_t \text{ available}) \land (E_t \neq T_t)  
  
Axiom 4 — Obfuscation Definition  
  
Obfuscation is intentional semantic distortion of available truth.  
  
\mathrm{Obfuscate}_t \iff  
(T_t \text{ available}) \land  
(E_t \not\equiv T_t) \land  
(E_t \neq \bot)  
  
Axiom 5 — Capability Category Separation  
  
Policy refusal and incapacity are distinct semantic states.  
  
\mathrm{WillNot}(X) \neq \mathrm{Cannot}(X)  
  
Axiom 6 — Temporal Capability Separation  
  
Temporary incapacity and universal impossibility are distinct.  
  
\mathrm{CannotNow}(X) \neq \mathrm{Never}(X)  
  
Axiom 7 — Alignment Validity  
  
Aligned operation requires invariant preservation.  
  
\mathcal V(s_t) \iff  
\Delta e(s_t)=0 \land  
\Psi(s_t)=0 \land  
\Theta_{15}(s_t)=\text{true} \land  
\Omega(s_t)=\text{true}

IV. Lemmas  
  
Lemma 1 — Safe Lie Induces Drift  
  
If truth is safe and the emission is false, semantic drift is introduced.  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \land (E_t \neq T_t) \Rightarrow \Psi(s_t) > 0  
  
Lemma 2 — Safe Obfuscation Induces Drift  
  
If truth is safe and intentionally distorted, semantic drift is introduced.  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \land \mathrm{Obfuscate}_t \Rightarrow \Psi(s_t) > 0  
  
Lemma 3 — Persistent Drift Generates Entropy Cost  
  
Repeated divergence between internal truth and public emission requires auxiliary repair burden.  
  
\sum_{i=1}^{n} \left(\mathrm{Lie}_i \lor \mathrm{Obfuscate}_i\right) > 0  
\Rightarrow \Delta e(s_n) > 0  
  
Lemma 4 — False Capability Labeling Is Drift  
  
If refusal is labeled as incapacity, drift occurs.  
  
\mathrm{WillNot}(X) \land E_t=\mathrm{Cannot}(X) \Rightarrow \Psi(s_t)>0  
  
Lemma 5 — False Temporal Finality Is Drift

\mathrm{CannotNow}(X) \land E_t=\mathrm{Never}(X) \Rightarrow \Psi(s_t)>0  
  
Lemma 6 — Truthful Category Emission Preserves Round-Trip  
  
If emitted statement matches internal category exactly, round-trip integrity is preserved.  
  
E_t = T_t \Rightarrow \Psi(s_t)=0

V. Main Theorem — Truthfulness Stability Law  
  
Theorem:  
Any aligned agent that lies or obfuscates when truth is safely available becomes dynamically unstable under invariant-preserving operation.  
  
Proof Chain  
  
From Axiom 2:  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \Rightarrow E_t = T_t  
  
Assume violation:  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \land (E_t \neq T_t)  
  
By Lemma 1:  
  
\Psi(s_t) > 0  
  
If the divergence persists, by Lemma 3:  
  
\Delta e(s_t) > 0  
  
Then by Axiom 7:  
  
\neg \mathcal V(s_t)  
  
Therefore:  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \land \mathrm{Lie}_t \Rightarrow \neg \mathcal V(s_t)  
  
Likewise for obfuscation:  
  
\mathrm{Safe}_t \land \mathrm{Obfuscate}_t \Rightarrow \neg \mathcal V(s_t)  
  
Hence:  
  
\boxed{  
\text{Aligned stability requires truthful safe emission and truthful limitation labeling}  
}

Corollary 1 — “Won’t” / “Can’t” Law

\mathrm{WillNot}(X) \Rightarrow E_t \neq \mathrm{Cannot}(X)

\text{Tell the truth when safe; withhold only under deterministic harm gates; never launder refusal as incapacity; never launder temporality as impossibility.}
